0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\textstyle\varphi\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptstyle\varphi\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptscriptstyle\varphi\hfil$\crcr}}}\right)(\boldsymbol{x})=\sum\limits_{k=1}^{N}\frac{\mathrm{e}^{i\omega|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{c}_{k}|}}{4\pi|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{c}_{k}|}c_{k}^{\varepsilon}\varphi_{k},

and using bounds (
E.6
) along with (
6.1
), we quantify the error between the two models via

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\hat{u}^{\varepsilon}_{B,\operatorname{app}}-\hat{u}^{\varepsilon}_{s,\operatorname{app}}\|_{L^{\infty}(K)}&\leq\|\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{B}\|_{\mathbb{C}^{N}\rightarrow L^{\infty}(K)}\|\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\displaystyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\displaystyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\textstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\textstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\textstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptscriptstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}_{s}^{\varepsilon}-\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\displaystyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\displaystyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\textstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\textstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\textstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptscriptstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}_{B}^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathbb{C}^{N}}+\|\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{B}-\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\|_{\mathbb{C}^{N}\rightarrow L^{\infty}(K)}\|\mathchoice{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\displaystyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\displaystyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\displaystyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\textstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\textstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\textstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}{\vbox{\halign{#\cr\kern-0.7pt\cr$\mkern 2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraitd}$}}}\mkern-1.5mu\leaders{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\mkern 0.0mu\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\montraita}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\montraita}$}}}\mkern 0.0mu$}}{\hfill}\mkern-1.5mu\fldr$\crcr\kern-0.3pt\cr$\hfil\scriptscriptstyle\lambda\hfil$\crcr}}}_{s}^{\varepsilon}\|_{\mathbb{C}^{N}}\\
&\leq C(N,\underline{d}_{*}^{\varepsilon},|\omega|,\operatorname{Im}\omega)\left[\|\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{B}\|_{\mathbb{C}^{N}\rightarrow L^{\infty}(K)}\left(\varepsilon^{-1}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{g}}^{\varepsilon,(2)}-\hat{\boldsymbol{g}}^{\varepsilon,(1)}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{\varepsilon})}+\|\hat{\boldsymbol{g}}^{\varepsilon,(1)}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{\varepsilon})}\right)\right.\\
&\left.+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{B}-\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{s}\|_{\mathbb{C}^{N}\rightarrow L^{\infty}(K)}\|\hat{\boldsymbol{g}}^{\varepsilon,(1)}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{\varepsilon})}\right],\end{split}

(E.8)

where
C(N,\underline{d}_{*}^{\varepsilon},|\omega|,\operatorname{Im}\omega)>0
independent of
\varepsilon
.
Next, we introduce a counterpart of Lemma
6.1
.

Lemma E.2

(Approximating the volume operator)
.

Let
K\subset\Omega^{c}
be a compact set. There exists
C_{K}>0
, s.t. the following bounds hold true for all
0<\varepsilon<1
,
\omega\in\mathbb{C}^{+}
:

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{B}\|_{\mathcal{L}(\mathbb{C}^{N},L^{\infty}(K))}\leq\varepsilon C_{K}N^{1/2},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{s}-\mathcal{S}^{\varepsilon}_{B}\|_{\mathcal{L}(\mathbb{C}^{N},L^{\infty}(K))}\leq\varepsilon^{2}N^{1/2}C_{K}(1+|\omega|).

Proof.

The first bound is obtained by the
\ell_{2}
Cauchy-Schwarz inequality together with
c^{\varepsilon}_{k}=\varepsilon c_{k}^{1}
, see (
4.20
). The second bound is obtained like in Lemma
6.1
, and thus we leave its proof for the reader.
∎

Lemma E.3
.

Let
\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}\in H^{3}(B)
. Then, there exists
C>0
such that for all
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\hat{\boldsymbol{g}}^{\varepsilon,(2)}-\hat{\boldsymbol{g}}^{\varepsilon,(1)}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{\varepsilon})}\leq\varepsilon^{2}\times CN^{1/2}\|\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}\|_{H^{3}(B)}.

Proof.

We remark that

\displaystyle\varphi_{k}(\boldsymbol{x}):=\hat{g}^{\varepsilon,(2)}_{k}(\boldsymbol{x})-\hat{g}^{\varepsilon,(1)}_{k}(\boldsymbol{x})=\nabla\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}(\boldsymbol{c}_{k})\cdot(\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{c}_{k}),\quad\boldsymbol{x}\in\Gamma^{\varepsilon}_{k}.

which yields

\displaystyle\|\varphi_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{\varepsilon}_{k})}\leq\|\nabla\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}\|_{L^{\infty}(B_{k})}\|\boldsymbol{x}-\boldsymbol{c}_{k}\|_{L^{2}(\Gamma^{\varepsilon}_{k})}\leq C\varepsilon^{2}\|\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}\|_{H^{3}(B)},

where the last inequality holds true by the Sobolev embedding
[
3
, Theorem 4.12, part 2]
, in particular, we have that
\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}\in C^{1,\alpha}(\overline{B})
,
\alpha<1/2
. Hence, the desired bound follows immediately.
∎

Combining (
E.8
) with Lemma
E.2
,
E.3
and Proposition
D.1
, we obtain the following result.

Theorem E.1
.

Let
K\subset B^{c}
be compact;
\omega\in\mathbb{C}^{+}
and
\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}(\omega)\in H^{3}(B)
. The error between
\hat{u}^{\varepsilon}_{s,\operatorname{app}}
and
\hat{u}^{\varepsilon}_{B,\operatorname{app}}
, defined in (
6.69
) and (
E.7
), is bounded by

\displaystyle\|\hat{u}^{\varepsilon}_{s,\operatorname{app}}-\hat{u}^{\varepsilon}_{B,\operatorname{app}}\|_{L^{\infty}(K)}\leq\varepsilon^{2}\times C_{K}C_{\operatorname{geom}}(N,\underline{d}_{*}^{\varepsilon})\,p(|\omega|,\operatorname{Im}\omega)\|\hat{u}^{\operatorname{inc}}\|_{H^{3}(B)},

where
C_{K}>0
depends on
K
.

Proof of Theorem
2.1
for the model (
2.18
)

Finally, to prove the desired statement of Theorem
2.1
for the Born model, we follow the lines of the proof of Theorem
2.1
for the model (
2.17
), see Section
6.2.7
, employing Theorem
E.1
.
Since the model (
2.17
) is convergent of order
p=2
, we conclude about the validity of the statement of Theorem
2.1
for the model (
2.18
).
